846 entries, criar-ui-sveltekit to critic. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **criar-ui-sveltekit**: Constrói interface em SvelteKit 2 com Svelte 5 (runes), TypeScript estrito e os tokens do docs/DESIGN.md: rotas, carregamento de dados no servidor, ações de formulário com aprimoramento progressivo, componentes com props tipadas e testes com Vitest e Playwright. Aciona ao criar página, rota, componente ou formulário em projeto SvelteKit. NÃO usar para decidir a stack (→ definir-stack), para definir cor, tipografia ou token (→ contrato-de-design), nem para lógica de servidor sem interface. ([psiagoleal/ai-coding-agent-profiles](https://github.com/psiagoleal/ai-coding-agent-profiles/tree/HEAD/skills/stack/criar-ui-sveltekit) · ★1)

- **crier**: Cross-post blog content to social platforms. Claude handles audit, user selection, and generates rewrites for short-form platforms. ([queelius/crier](https://github.com/queelius/crier/tree/HEAD/src/crier) · ★20)
- **crier**: Cross-post blog content to platforms with the crier MCP tools. Publishes one file (pass its path) or finds what needs cross-posting; holds the canonical rules for rewrites, approval, paste/import platforms, and failure recovery. ([queelius/claude-anvil](https://github.com/queelius/claude-anvil/tree/HEAD/crier/skills/crier) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **crif**: Interactive crypto deep-research framework with human-AI collaboration for superior research outcomes ([modbender/skill-library-mcp](https://github.com/modbender/skill-library-mcp/tree/HEAD/data/crif) · ★21 · 6 copies)
- **crim-data-analysis**: Use when executing and reporting the analysis for a Criminology (ASC / Wiley) manuscript so it survives expert review — honest uncertainty, robustness, and methods appropriate to crime counts, longitudinal panels, trajectory models, and recidivism survival. Guides analysis norms; it does not fabricate results. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Criminology-Skills/skills/crim-data-analysis) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **crim-data-and-transparency**: Use when preparing the data, replication, and transparency materials for a Criminology (ASC / Wiley) manuscript — data-availability statements, reproducibility packages, preregistration, and handling restricted criminal-justice data under DUAs. Criminology operates under growing open-science norms (Wiley open-science badges, ICPSR/NACJD archiving). Prepares the package; it does not waive requirements. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Criminology-Skills/skills/crim-data-and-transparency) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **crim-literature-positioning**: Use when positioning a Criminology (ASC / Wiley) manuscript against the literature so it reads as a contribution to the field's live debates, not a citation pile. Criminology readers expect engagement with the relevant theoretical tradition and the closest prior empirical work across the field's many disciplines. Stakes the contribution; it does not write the lit review. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Criminology-Skills/skills/crim-literature-positioning) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **crim-rebuttal**: Use when writing the response to a Criminology (ASC / Wiley) revise-and-resubmit. Criminology revisions usually require converting multiple expert reviewers across the field's disciplines while keeping the editor confident the revision is convergent and the theoretical contribution intact. Structures the response letter; it does not fabricate new results. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Criminology-Skills/skills/crim-rebuttal) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **crim-research-design**: Use when defending the research design of a Criminology (ASC / Wiley) manuscript — causal identification for quantitative work, longitudinal and life-course designs, criminal-career and trajectory methods, place-based and experimental designs, or case selection and process tracing for qualitative work. Criminology judges each tradition on its own terms. Strengthens the design; it does not write code. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Criminology-Skills/skills/crim-research-design) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **crim-review-process**: Use to understand how Criminology (ASC / Wiley) evaluates a manuscript — blinded/anonymized peer review, editorial screening, the typical reject / major-revision / minor-revision / accept outcomes, and what expert reviewers weigh in this interdisciplinary, theory-forward field. Sets expectations and shapes the paper to survive review; it does not contact editors. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Criminology-Skills/skills/crim-review-process) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **crim-submission**: Use when running the final pre-submission preflight for Criminology (ASC / Wiley) via the official online submission link — anonymized main document plus separate title page, APA-based formatting, bias-free language, double-anonymized review, single-submission commitment, Wiley ACT ORCID/OA/preprint fields, and live length/abstract caps. Final checks; it does not draft content. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Criminology-Skills/skills/crim-submission) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **crim-submission**: Use when running the final pre-submission preflight for Criminology (ASC / Wiley) via the Wiley Research Exchange portal — anonymized main document plus separate title page, APA-based formatting, bias-free language, single-submission commitment, and (待核实) length/abstract caps and ORCID. Final checks; it does not draft content. ([franklee16/academic-research-skills](https://github.com/franklee16/academic-research-skills/tree/HEAD/Awesome-Journal-Skills-main/Criminology-Skills/skills/crim-submission) · ★240)
- **crim-tables-figures**: Use when building tables and figures for a Criminology (ASC / Wiley) manuscript so exhibits are self-contained, accessible, and communicate crime patterns clearly — age-crime curves, trajectory-group plots, recidivism survival curves, hot-spot maps, and effect plots. Designs exhibits; it does not run the analysis. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Criminology-Skills/skills/crim-tables-figures) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **crim-theory-building**: Use when building the theoretical argument of a Criminology (ASC / Wiley) manuscript into a field-level contribution. Criminology rewards an explicit mechanism, scope conditions, and observable implications over a bare crime correlation, whether the work tests, extends, or adjudicates a criminological theory. Structures the argument; it does not run analyses. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Criminology-Skills/skills/crim-theory-building) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **crim-topic-selection**: Use when deciding whether a project fits Criminology (ASC / Wiley) and whether to target a full Article or a Research Note. Criminology is the discipline's interdisciplinary flagship, so the test is a general criminological contribution about the etiology, patterning, or control of crime — not a dataset-specific correlation. Helps frame the question; it does not collect data. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Criminology-Skills/skills/crim-topic-selection) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **crim-workflow**: Use as the entry point for any Criminology (ASC / Wiley) manuscript. Routes to the right crim sub-skill based on where you are in the lifecycle and whether the paper is a full Article or a Research Note. Criminology is theory-forward and interdisciplinary, so the router first checks that the paper makes a criminological contribution, not just reports a crime correlation. It dispatches; it does not draft content. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Criminology-Skills/skills/crim-workflow) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **crim-writing-style**: Use when drafting or polishing a Criminology (ASC / Wiley) manuscript so it reads for an interdisciplinary criminology audience, follows the journal's APA-based style, and uses bias-free, person-first language for justice-involved populations. Tightens prose and format; it does not invent content. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Criminology-Skills/skills/crim-writing-style) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)

- **criminal-defense**: 刑事辩护全流程工具集，按需求路由生成各类辩护工作产品。触发词包括：辩护意见、法律意见书、辩护词、质证提纲、庭审提纲、发问提纲、会见提纲、取保候审、变更强制措施、阅卷笔录、卷宗梳理、非法证据排除、认罪认罚、量刑分析、刑期计算、刑事和解、谅解书、缓刑、上诉、二审、申诉再审、被害人代理、附带民事、涉案财物等。仅用于刑事辩护与刑事代理场景，不适用于民事纠纷、合同审查、行政案件。 ([FAYANHUIYING/claude-for-legal-HoriZon](https://github.com/FAYANHUIYING/claude-for-legal-HoriZon/tree/HEAD/criminal-legal/skills/criminal-defense) · ★2)

Use whenever the user asks to draft, rewrite, tighten, or improve a procedural document in a criminal case. - **criminal-defense-mindset**: name: criminal-defense-strategy display_name: Z律-刑事辩护策略_V7.0 author: Z律同行 version: "7.0.0" created: "2026-06-17" description: "【V7.0最终版】五轮迭代打磨（V3/V2原始比较→V4融合框架→V5规则内嵌→V6罪名全景+量刑文书深化→V7最终审查修复）。Part 6「罪名体系全景图」以双轴分类法（犯罪场域×侵害法益）为理论基底，覆盖15类高发重点刑事案件（近5年趋势+未来5-10年预判+政策信号+证据特征+四维突破口），15类同层级并列。6个最高频罪名配有完整六环节辩护链。Part 4量刑计算精确化（法发〔2021〕21号参数内嵌+完整计算示例）。Part 5 10种文书/庭审模板（7种文书+发问提纲+法官提问预判15题）。新增防御性证据体系、追诉时效审查、极简降 ([CSlawyer1985/legal-skillhub](https://github.com/CSlawyer1985/legal-skillhub/tree/HEAD/skills/criminal-defense-mindset) · ★37)
- **criminal-defense-speech-trial**: 生成刑事一审辩护词或开庭后提交法院的书面辩护意见。触发：一审辩护词、庭后书面意见、补充辩护意见、法庭辩论稿。不触发：二审文书调用 criminal-appeal-second-instance-drafting；审查起诉意见调用 criminal-prosecution-defense-opinion-drafting。 ([ahang1598/doubao-workbuddy-qwenwork-skills](https://github.com/ahang1598/doubao-workbuddy-qwenwork-skills/tree/HEAD/workbuddy/experts/criminal-defense-expert/skills/criminal-defense-speech-trial) · ★103 · 2 copies)
- **criminal-defense-strategy**: Criminal Defense Strategy Advisor for defense counsel. Answers "what do we do on this case", "which line of defense", "should we appeal and where", "how do we break this evidence", "plead or fight", "how do we get bail or release", "what are the chances". Reads the case materials (charging documents, disclosure, statements, expert reports, scans), builds a case map and evidence table, offers 2–3 lines of defense with prospects and price, a step plan, and an appeal map with deadlines. Jurisdiction-neutral with a common-law lens (disclosure, suppression, bail, plea bargaining, sentencing, appea… ([arsenyajurist-sudo/legal-skills](https://github.com/arsenyajurist-sudo/legal-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/criminal-defense-strategy/skills/criminal-defense-strategy) · ★1)
- **criminal-defense-strategy**: Develops defense theories, probable cause challenges, suppression issues, witness impeachment, trial strategy, and remedy selection, and reviews prior counsel's performance. ([allenbenj/pages-main](https://github.com/allenbenj/pages-main/tree/HEAD/.github/criminal-defense-strategy) · 2 copies)
- **criminal-defense-workflow**: 顶级刑事律师全流程辩护技能。覆盖刑事案件辩护的完整生命周期：会见接待、强制措施变更、阅卷分析、 证据质证、庭前会议、庭审准备、辩护词撰写、上诉申诉等全流程环节。 当用户涉及刑事案件辩护工作时触发，包括但不限于："帮我阅卷分析刑事案件""写质证意见""做证据分析报告" "撰写辩护词""为刑事案件辩护""分析案卷材料""刑事辩护全流程""会见笔录整理""取保候审申请" "庭前会议准备""庭审发问提纲""上诉状撰写"等。 适用于各类刑事案件，尤其擅长经济犯罪、职务犯罪等证据密集型案件。 ([CSlawyer1985/legal-skillhub](https://github.com/CSlawyer1985/legal-skillhub/tree/HEAD/skills/criminal-defense-workflow) · ★37)

- **criterion-self-evidence-check**: 判据自证性检查卡：凡形如「当 X 满足条件 C，就判定 X 属于类别 Y」的规则，若 C 的证据集里装着 X 自己（或装着判据作者自己写死的硬编码串），这条判据就永远成立、永远不可证伪——它测的是同义反复，不是事实。给出两条可复用探针（挖除探针 / 跨样本重复度探针）与三类修法。实测三个真案例：押韵判据把目标字写进韵表、模板检测器被生成器改一个字绕过、同义反复测试的断言抄了设计者的决定。触发词：判据、分类器、启发式、规则命中、白名单、证据集、自证、循环论证、同义反复、模板检测、通配误判、规则永远成立、误伤率降了但还有漏网、跨篇重复、阴性对照。不适用：纯格式校验（长度/字符集/必填）没有"证据集"概念；业务上本就该自指的规则（如 id 唯一性是定义不是循环）。 ([kumu314/Agent-Evals](https://github.com/kumu314/Agent-Evals/tree/HEAD/t2/skills/criterion-self-evidence-check) · ★1)
